### Fraction comparison proof contract — 5 November 2026

**Qualified for `Fraction::compare` only; final evidence below.** This is enclosure-inventory row 2,
under the same OD-10/CHECK-7/8/25 owner. Independent source/algebra review
approved the following contract after constructor qualification. No comparison
compiler or solver command had run at that contract freeze; final results follow.

Cover every pair satisfying the complete checked fraction invariant, including
unreduced representations. Require success throughout and exactly the ordering
of `n/2^s` and `m/2^t`; no error variant is allowed on this domain. Generate four
unrestricted raw inputs and call the actual constructor for each side. The
completed admission/retention theorem establishes that every valid representation
is reached; constructor rejection must not become an unexamined vacuity assumption.
Keep actual constructor/type/constant/comparison bodies source-bound.

The independent oracle handles zero first, then equal denominators by numerator
ordering. For `s < t`, put `d = t-s`: when `d < 64`, compare
`u128(n) << d` against `u128(m)`; otherwise return Greater. Mirror the expression
and return Less for a large gap when `s > t`. The shifted oracle value has at
most 116 bits. With a gap of at least 64, the positive smaller-denominator side
is at least `2^64` after cross scaling while the other numerator is below `2^53`.
No enormous denominator or production leading-exponent oracle is copied.

Machine obligations also establish that branch-directed subtraction and i64
leading-exponent arithmetic fit. Positive bit lengths are in `1..=53`, leading
exponents in `[-1040,0]`. Equal leading exponents imply `t-s = c-b`, so any
actual alignment shifts by at most 52 and retains at most 53 bits. This
nontruncation result is required: Rust `checked_shl` checks the count, not
discarded high bits. Unequal leading exponents must imply strict rational order.

The planned finite checkpoint is healthy, three compiled defects and restored
healthy: unconditional Invariant at `(1,1)` versus `(1,1)` must lose required
success; reversed unequal-leading result at `(1,2)` versus `(1,1)` must lose Less;
zeroing the first alignment count at `(1,1)` versus `(2,2)` must lose Equal while
preserving a valid Ordering codomain. Retain the existing unwind-1/default
safety/sanity/resource limits; source/harness review and formatting precede freeze.

Twenty nonvacuity covers use `A=2^52`, `B=2^53-1`: zero/zero; zero versus
`(1,1041)` in both directions; `(1,1)` versus itself; `(1,2)`/`(1,1)` in both
directions; `(2,3)`/`(3,3)` in both directions; `(1,1)`/`(2,2)` in both directions;
`(1,1)`/`(A,53)` in both directions; `(1,1)`/`(A+1,53)` in both directions;
`(A,53)`/`(A,116)` in both directions; `(A,53)`/`(A,117)` in both directions;
`(B,1041)` versus `(B,53)`; and `(1,1)` versus `(1,1041)`. These exercise zeros,
strict order/equality, unreduced equivalence, both maximum-alignment branches,
wide oracle gap 63, shortcut gap 64 and maximum numerator/denominator bounds.
They do not replace the complete symbolic domain. Complement/addition,
decomposition, caller admission and whole enclosure remain open.

### Fraction arithmetic proof contract — 5 November 2026

**Complement qualified; addition incomplete after timeout.** Row 3 comprises bounded complement
and addition. Two independent read-only algebra/source reviews approved the
following oracles. Actual constructor, comparison and arithmetic bodies remain
source-bound; no callee stub or Kani input assumption is permitted.

Use actual constructors with independent raw-completeness checks, retaining the
qualified retention/surjectivity argument. Every oracle reads the **constructed
canonical fields**, not raw input shifts. Raw `(0,1041)` constructs `(0,0)`:
using its original shift would falsely classify two zeros as a positive small sum.

For complement, require success iff the checked fraction is at least one half.
Independently evaluate `1 <= shift <= 53 && 2*u128(numerator) >= 2^shift`, guarding
the power before evaluation. Success retains the exact shift and satisfies
`output.numerator + input.numerator = 2^shift`, with a nonzero proper output
numerator at most `2^52`. Every other checked input returns exactly `Invariant`.
Nonzero representations remain unreduced when supplied that way.

For addition, require success on every checked pair. Let
`M=max(left.shift,right.shift)` from the actual constructed fractions:

- If `M<128`, independently align both numerators in u128 to denominator
  `D=2^M`. Each aligned value is below `D`, so their sum fits even at `M=127`
  (at most `2^128-2`). Carry iff the sum is at least `D`; residual iff the sum
  is neither zero nor `D`. Prove alignment has no lost bits and addition fits.
- If `M>=128`, at least one fraction is positive and below `2^-75`. Every
  checked fraction is at most `1-2^-53`; therefore the exact sum is strictly
  between zero and one, requiring `(carry,residual)=(false,true)`. The guarded
  integer oracle forms no oversized denominator. This branch still checks the
  actual arithmetic source on every admitted large-shift pair.

### Scaled-value split proof contract — 5 November 2026

**Qualified through the [terminal split checkpoint](#split-terminal-qualification--5-november-2026).**
Two independent source/algebra reviews approved this row-4 continuation before
preparation. It introduces no addition retry or verifier work.
Actual `ScaledValue::split(u64, i64)` succeeds iff `significand<2^53` and
`-1041<=scale<=11`; all other raw inputs return exactly `Invariant`, even zeros
with unsupported scales. Keep the actual fraction constructor and zero constant.

Gate all oracle arithmetic on that independent admission predicate before negating
scale. For nonnegative scale, compare widened whole with exact widened left
scaling and require canonical zero fraction. For negative scale put `k=-scale`:

- For `k<64`, require `(u128(whole)<<k)+fraction.numerator=significand` and
  `fraction.numerator<2^k`. These uniquely characterize Euclidean quotient and
  remainder without copying the source's cutoff-53/mask algorithm or dividing.
  Even arbitrary faulty u64 output fields give a reconstruction of at most
  `2^127+2^63-1`, strictly below `2^128`, so the oracle itself cannot overflow.
- For `k>=64`, require whole zero and fraction numerator equal to significand.
  The admitted 53-bit input is below the denominator. Nonzero fractions retain
  shift `k`; every zero fraction canonicalizes to `(0,0)` in either branch.

Raw split's maximum whole is `2^64-2048`, attained at `(2^53-1,11)`. Its tuple
return must not inherit `ScaledValue`'s narrower `2^63` struct-field bound.
`from_bits` separately admits exactly magnitude bits at most
`0x41d0_0000_0000_0000` (both signs of finite magnitude at most `2^30`), thereby
establishing that struct bound. Its positive endpoint exceeds the later validated
operand domain; neither from-bits admission nor joint Garde admission is proved
by split. The independent signed-whole helper also retains its separate obligation.

Plan five sequential jobs: healthy full domain; reject-all at `(1,0)`; valid but
wrong remainder at `(3,-1)` returning `(1,0,0)`; wrong Invariant classification
at `(0,12)`; restored healthy. Twenty covers exercise zeros at scale 0/-1041/-64,
exact `(1,0)`, maximum `(2^53-1,11)`, exact/fractional neighbors at -1,
cutoffs -52/-53 and -63/-64, maximum fraction shift -1041, oversized significands,
unsupported scales 12/-1042 including zero, and both i64 extremes.
Keep the existing admission/safety/sanity/unwind/resource profile and complete
source/harness review before execution. Addition and the remaining row-4/5/6
source consumers stay open; no global mathematical or production acceptance follows.

### Scaled-value representation proof contracts — 5 November 2026

**Both leaves qualified through the
[terminal representation checkpoint](#representation-terminal-qualification--6-november-2026).**
Two independent source/algebra reviews approved these row-4 contracts before
preparation. Preserve the actual
`MAX_SCALED_MAGNITUDE`, both methods, `split`, `Fraction::new`, `ZERO`, private
structs and typed errors. Reuse split's completed evidence without assuming away
the actual callee body in this source-projection route. No application-body or
verifier change is proposed.

For `from_bits(bits: u64)`, require success exactly when
`(bits & 0x7fff_ffff_ffff_ffff) <= 0x41d0_0000_0000_0000`, and exactly `Invariant`
otherwise. This covers every raw bit pattern, both signs of finite magnitude
through `2^30`, and both signed zeros; it is broader than later Garde operand
admission. On success retain the original sign bit, whole at most `2^63`, proper
fraction invariants, canonical zero and exact represented Q33 magnitude.

Use the independent binary64 definition: from sign-cleared magnitude put
`E=magnitude/2^52`, `M=magnitude%2^52`, `N=M+(E!=0 ? 2^52 : 0)`, `A=max(E,1)`.
The represented magnitude in Q33 quanta is `N*2^(A-1042)`, where
`1042=1023+52-33`. Gate oracle arithmetic on the independent raw admission
predicate. Compute `k=1042-A` in **signed i64**, avoiding unsigned subtraction.
For `k<=0`, require widened exact whole scaling and a canonical zero fraction.
For positive `k<64`, require `(u128(whole)<<k)+numerator=N` and `numerator<2^k`;
even arbitrary faulty u64 outputs give at most `2^127+2^63-1`, strictly below
`2^128`. For `k>=64`, require
whole zero and numerator `N`. Nonzero fractions retain shift `k`; zero is `(0,0)`.
This uses neither huge denominators nor the source's cutoff-53/mask oracle.
Retain the source's unreduced representation, including numerator `2^52`, shift
53 for half a Q33 quantum. Do not use the production maximum constant as the
independent admission oracle.

Three compiled body controls: change subnormal scale `-1041` to `-1040` at raw
bits 1 (exact numerical/shift failure); force the sign field false at negative
zero (original-sign failure); change the final magnitude-guard error to
`Unrepresentable` at `0x41d0_0000_0000_0001` (exact rejection classification).
The healthy theorem must separately require success, so rejection-only behavior
cannot pass. Twenty covers use these explicit raw patterns:

```text
0000000000000000 8000000000000000 0000000000000001 8000000000000001
000fffffffffffff 0010000000000000 3dd0000000000000 3ddfffffffffffff
3de0000000000000 3de0000000000001 3d30000000000000 3d20000000000000
41d0000000000000 c1d0000000000000 41cfffffffffffff 41d0000000000001
7ff0000000000000 fff0000000000000 7ff8000000000001 fff0000000000001
```

For `signed_whole`, prove the stronger raw domain of a bool sign, any u64 whole
and arbitrary primitive fraction fields. This extends the private proof domain;
it grants no unchecked construction permission to product callers. Require
`result.unsigned_abs()==u128(whole)`, `result<0 iff negative && whole!=0`,
`result==0 iff whole==0`, and independence from both fraction fields. Unsigned
absolute value remains safe even for a faulty `i128::MIN` result. The raw result
envelope is `[-(2^64-1), +(2^64-1)]`; whole at most `2^63` narrows it to
`[-2^63,+2^63]`, including positive `2^63`. Never narrow the theorem to i64.

Controls: omit negation at `(whole=1,negative=true)`; narrow through i64 at
`(u64::MAX,false)`; add the fractional numerator to the positive result at
`(0,false,Fraction{numerator:1,shift:1})`, which must still produce zero.
Twenty covers pair each sign with whole
`0,1,2,2^63-1,2^63,2^63+1,2^64-2,2^64-1` using zero fraction (sixteen), then both
signs of whole zero with fraction `(1,1)`, positive whole one with raw fraction
`(u64::MAX,0)` and negative whole one with `(0,u32::MAX)` (four). Independently
check their expected values during preparation before native execution.

### Ordinary addition partition assessment — 6 November 2026

The independent source review supports a new **ordinary-Kani** feasibility
hypothesis after the distinct terminal monolithic timeout and contract-mode
compatibility failure. Keep every actual constructor/comparison/complement/addition
body inline, the original independent `addition_contract`, all admitted raw
u64/u32/u64/u32 inputs, checked numerators, unreduced representations and shifts
through 1041. Use no function contract, callee stub or assumed product theorem.
Partition membership is the only proof-local domain condition, discharged for
the full theorem by a separate coverage/disjointness proof and **every** leaf.
This is not a qualified theorem or an execution result.

For raw numerator/shift pairs, use the independently reviewed bit-length factory
admission predicate. For classification only, canonicalize a zero numerator's
shift to zero; do not rewrite product input or call an alternative constructor.
For an admitted canonical fraction define `L(n,s)` by
`1 <= s <= 53 && 2*u128(n) >= 2^s`, guarding the finite power first. Let `A` mean
both raw inputs are admitted. The six guards are:

| Case | Complete raw-input condition |
| --- | --- |
| 0 | `!A`; at least one actual factory must reject. |
| 1 | `A && !L(left) && !L(right)`. |
| 2 | `A && L(left) && canonical_right_shift >= 106`. |
| 3 | `A && L(left) && canonical_right_shift < 106`. |

#### Concrete refinement argument to review — 6 November 2026

A possible manual-composition bridge must be stronger than a statement that the
models look reasonable. For each selected pure callee `f_i` over its complete
admitted domain `D_i`, require an ordinary actual-body machine proof of both:

```text
x in D_i => Post_i(x, f_i(x))
x in D_i => decode_i(encode_i(f_i(x))) == f_i(x)
```

Here `encode_i` is a total constructive seed witness for the actual return, and
`decode_i` is the exact return generator used by the model. Its seed consists
only of complete primitive `kani::any` fields, with exhaustive typed enum
encoding. Proving decode/encode only on already generated model outputs would
be insufficient; the second obligation must start from the **actual callee's
return for every admitted input**. Equality must preserve exact fields and error/
Ordering variants, with discriminating wrong-seed/return controls and source
review of the observation relation. This avoids claiming completeness from
selected boundary examples or an unreviewed `Arbitrary` implementation.

A model may then generate an unrestricted seed, decode it, assert its exact
input precondition and restrict the return only by `Post_i`. The order must
preserve every precondition assertion before any postcondition assumption; raw
output generation itself grants no checked-object authority. The inclusion
argument is constructive:

```text
M_i(x) = { decode_i(seed) | seed in S_i and Post_i(x, decode_i(seed)) }
actual-body post + actual-return seed witness => f_i(x) belongs to M_i(x)
```

If the unchanged finite caller body proves its original property for every
permitted model choice, checks all callee preconditions, and every actual callee
return belongs to that model set, the standard pure-call refinement rule can
relate the composed proof to actual execution. This rule/controller is an
explicit trusted assurance layer; Kani's ordinary stub feature does not certify
it. No effect, mutation, alias/lifetime, nondeterminism or termination premise
may be silently omitted. For these three candidate callees the complete selected
source must justify the pure, terminating Copy/value-only frame.

The intended actual-body refinement proofs retain original raw factories,
independent constructor/comparison/complement obligations and reachable cases;
the new `Post_i` functions receive their own proof, not just old receipt names.
`Fraction::new` has no precondition; compare needs two canonical checked
fractions; complement admits **all** canonical checked fractions, including its
zero/below-half/high-shift rejection regions. Do not strengthen complement's
precondition to successful cases. No caller data or model may normalize raw
arguments or drop unreduced/extreme-shift cases.

Before any implementation card, independently review whether this constructive
inclusion proof, purity/frame argument, exact replacement mappings and sealed
callee certificates actually meet the existing full-source acceptance policy.
Then specify a finite native mechanism/refinement/caller-control matrix, source
identities, fixed flags/resources and stop rule. Fresh callee success must precede
any caller substitution; expected-negative controls issue no certificates. The
central addition body/property remains actual and unassumed, with all eleven
labels, 23 witnesses, original three defects and complete restored input identity.
Absent those reviewed premises, this remains a model-only proposal and DEFER.

#### Additional native Ordering equality obligation

A bounded source-only follow-up explains an extra obligation beyond powers:
actual `Ordering == Ordering` must agree with ghost logical equality. Pinned
Verus rewrites ghost equality to `spec_eq`; executable datatype equality lowers
directly to SMT equality only for `Structural` types. The inspected builtin
implementations omit Ordering. Pinned `std_specs/core.rs` declares its external
type, while complete `std_specs/cmp.rs` supplies no Ordering equality model.

needs `eq_spec` connected to logical equality. A rational helper's `returns`
contract fixes the compared value, not the subsequent native trait operation.
